### 3c THE CHOOSER READ — one typed factor, at each site's own load point

⭐⭐ **THE LOAD IS PER-SITE ARITY — MONADIC WHERE THERE IS NO SUBJECT, DYADIC WHERE
THERE IS (the M5 closure).** The compile printed one universal two-term product and
also charged `anticipationFactorFor` with a `subjectId` argument that several joined
sites have nothing to bind. Both cannot be true, and an implementer building HB-4 would
have had to decide it alone. **RULED: the load has TWO SHAPES, chosen by whether the
decision has a COUNTERPART, and the choice is a declared field on each fork-registry
row (`arity: 'monadic' | 'dyadic'`), never an implementer's judgment at the seam.**

```js
// MONADIC — a decision with no counterpart. The habit factor alone.
habitLoad = habitFactorFor(worldState, actorId, cls, action)        // ×1 when dark/absent

// DYADIC — a decision taken AGAINST a named counterpart.
habitLoad = habitFactorFor(worldState, actorId, cls, action)
          * anticipationFactorFor(worldState, actorId, subjectId, cls, action)
```

⭐⭐ **AND ARITY IS PER-ACTION WHERE THE COUNTERPART EXISTS ONLY ON SOME ACTIONS — THE
P2 CLOSURE, AND IT IS A THIRD SHAPE, NOT A CAVEAT ON THE SECOND.** The amendment declared
site 7 DYADIC because *"the counterpart is `bestTargetId`, MEASURED present on every
emitted move"*. **REFUTED (R17).** MEASURED at HEAD, both of `enumerateMoves`' emission
paths are conditional on the move token — the reason-sidecar branch spells
`...(move === 'deploy' && bestTargetId != null ? { bestTargetId: String(bestTargetId) }
: {}),` and the plain branch `.map((move) => move === 'deploy' && bestTargetId != null ?
{ move, score: scored[move], bestTargetId: String(bestTargetId) } : { move, score:
scored[move] })` — and the return type was already honest (`bestTargetId?:string`,
OPTIONAL). So:

```js
// PER-ACTION — a vocabulary in which only SOME actions have a counterpart.
habitLoad(action) = habitFactorFor(worldState, actorId, cls, action)
                  * (dyadicActions.includes(action)
                       ? anticipationFactorFor(worldState, actorId, subjectIdFor(action), cls, action)
                       : 1)
```

**THE REGISTRY FIELD BECOMES `arity: 'monadic' | 'dyadic' | 'per-action'`, and a
`per-action` row carries a `dyadicActions: [...]` list that is walker-asserted against
the emitter's OWN conditional** — the same "the emitter is the denominator, never a hand
list" discipline HB-1 already applies to `STRATEGY_MOVES`. ⛔ **A `per-action` row whose
`dyadicActions` list is not derivable from an executed read of the emitter is a build
STOP**, because that list is exactly the claim that fell.

**THE ARITY TABLE — EVERY JOINED ROW, NO EXCEPTIONS (the P9 closure).** The amendment
ruled arity *"a declared field on each fork-registry row, never an implementer's judgment
at the seam"* and then left three joined sites without one, handing the decision back at
three seams. Sites 4, 5 and 14 no longer need an arity because they are STRUCK
(R13/R14/R15); the remaining rows are complete:

| Site | Arity | The measurement |
|---|---|---|
| 1 `demographicsResponses.selectResponse` | **MONADIC** | CONFIRMED: `export function selectResponse(input)` over `{ scored, realmId, settlementId, episode }` — no counterpart id in scope, and a settlement's own overflow decision has no counterparty to anticipate |
| 2 `generosityKernel`'s caller (⚠ NOT the leaf — see below) | **DYADIC** | CONFIRMED at the CALLER: `generosityKernel.js` iterates qualifying PAIRS and calls `const decision = refugeAcceptance({ ...motive, priorPosture, now: tick });`, pushing `{ giverId, receiverId, verdict, magnitude }`. ⚠ **THE LEAF CANNOT CARRY IT:** MEASURED, `src/domain/spatial/generosityEV.js` — `export function refugeAcceptance({ bond, history, conscience, strategy, faith, quadrantMod, lensMod, priorPosture, now } = { now: 0 })` — **no actor id and no counterpart id.** This is the SAME L2 defect class already corrected at site 10 and it is corrected here at the polish: the seam is the caller, the leaf stays pure |
| 3 `coalitionRatification` ballot | ⚠ **DYADIC — VERIFY-AT-BUILD, and the STOP is real** | `export function castRatificationBallot({ member, picture, termSheet } = {})` carries the voting member and the term sheet; whether the sheet exposes a stable OFFERER id at the load point is NOT executed here. If it does not, the row degrades to MONADIC — never to a phantom counterpart |
| 6 `tradeWar.evaluateTradeWar` → `contestOverThirdParty` | **DYADIC** | the per-supplier `scoreFor` is computed against a named supplier; the supplier IS the counterpart |
| ⭐ 7 `settlementStrategy` | ⭐ **PER-ACTION** — `dyadicActions: ['deploy']` | R17, MEASURED above. Ten of eleven moves are MONADIC at the load point |
| 8 `warPeaceDecision` | **DYADIC** | the opponent is in scope throughout — e.g. `export function inheritedWarDemandFor(worldState, actorId, opponentId, snapshot = null)` |
| 10 `advanceIntervention`'s caller | **DYADIC** | the caller holds both actor ids, which is why L2 moved the seam there |
| 12 `sovereigntyMarketStage` | ⚠ **MONADIC — VERIFY-AT-BUILD** | `export function offerWeightOf(score01, base = T.BASE_OFFER_WEIGHT)` takes no ids at all; the caller holds `courtId` and `assetId`, and **an ASSET IS NOT AN ACTOR TO ANTICIPATE** (J-HB-4). If a rival BIDDER is resolvable at the load point the row becomes DYADIC; absent that measurement it is MONADIC |
| 13 `npcLadderGoals.mintGoal` | **MONADIC** | `export function mintGoal({ npc, faction, rungIndex, sid, frame, item, weeks })` — no counterpart |

⚠ **A DYADIC (OR A DYADIC ACTION) WITH AN UNRESOLVED SUBJECT DEGRADES TO MONADIC, NOT TO
ZERO** — `anticipationFactorFor` returns exactly 1 for an absent `subjectId`, the same
neutral-identity door as a dark flag.

⭐⭐ **AND THE ARITY FIELD IS NOT THE WIRING FIELD — THE SECOND HALF OF P9, AND IT
RESOLVES THE CONTRADICTION WITH HB-7's CHARTER.** The amendment declared six sites DYADIC
while HB-7 wired only two seams, which left five sites either carrying a multiplicand no
wave ever makes ≠ 1 (a dead arm at five sites, undeclared) or receiving a term outside
HB-7's charter. **RULED: those are two different facts and they take two different
fields.**

```
arity              — CAN a counterpart bind here?  (measured from the signature/emitter)
anticipationWave   — IS an anticipation term wired here?  ('HB-7' | null)
```

**A `LEARN` row with `arity: 'dyadic'` and `anticipationWave: null` ships the ONE-TERM
form.** There is no second multiplicand in the expression at all — not a `1`, not a call
that returns `1`, **nothing** — so there is no dead arm to find later. ⚠ **THE `LEARN`
CLAUSE IS LOAD-BEARING AND WAS ADDED AT THE ATTESTATION (J-HB-26):** a DEFER row ships no
expression whatever its arity says, so "one-term" is not a description that can apply to
it.

⭐⭐ **AND THE SET OF SUCH ROWS IS DERIVED, NEVER HAND-LISTED — J-HB-25, "DERIVE, DON'T
ENUMERATE" — ⭐⭐ WITH THE DISPOSITION FILTER THE QUERY WAS MISSING (J-HB-26).** The
one-term set is DEFINED as *"every `habitForkRegistry.js` row **whose `disposition` is
`LEARN`** AND whose `arity` is `dyadic` AND whose `anticipationWave` is `null`"*. **That
query, run against the registry, IS the set.** ⛔ **This sentence is not the authority and
must never be read as one:** a hand-listed set is a second denominator, and a second
denominator goes stale the first time any row's arity is corrected — which is precisely
how this clause acquired its errors. ⚠ **Worked example ONLY, EXECUTED against the
disposition partition and the arity table at this revision: `{3, 6}`.**

⭐⭐ **WHY THE FILTER, AND WHY IT IS A CORRECTION TO THE QUERY RATHER THAN TO THE ANSWER
(J-HB-26).** The clause this set illustrates is about **SHIPPED EXPRESSION FORMS** — which
joined sites emit a habit load with ONE multiplicand in it and no second term. **A DEFER
row ships no expression at all.** It has no load point, no `habitLoad` product, and no
line of code for a second multiplicand to be absent from, so calling it "one-term" is a
category error: there is no term. The two-clause query returned `{2, 3, 6, 10}`, and sites
2 and 10 are DEFER (R18) — **the query was answering a question about registry METADATA
while the clause was asking a question about BUILT CODE.** ⚠ **AND THE DEFER ROWS KEEP
THEIR DECLARED ARITY, WHICH IS NOT A CONTRADICTION:** `arity` on a DEFER row is REGISTRY
METADATA recording the measured fact *"a counterpart CAN bind here"*, banked so that the
row's promotion at HB-9 does not have to re-measure it — **it becomes load-bearing at
promotion, when the row's close lands and the site starts shipping an expression.** A
DEFER row with no arity would make every promotion re-open a question this volume already
executed.

**THE INJECTION LAW — the site's own idiom, never a shared chokepoint.** MEASURED:
there is NO single weighted-pick chokepoint in this estate. There are FOUR idioms, a
habit helper cannot serve all four identically, **and each idiom has its own APPLICATION
LAW stating WHERE the load lands and WHAT the ratio pin (§3b) measures there:**

| Idiom | Sites | ⭐ APPLICATION LAW — where the factor enters | ⭐ What the §3b ODDS-RATIO pin measures here | Why it is replay-safe |

MEASURED-clean at the anchor: `settlementStrategy` computes `softmaxWeights(decisionMoves.map((m) => m.score), STRATEGY_K)` at the CALLER, outside `enumerateMoves`, so the seam is the returned `weights` array and no foreign scorer is touched | ⭐ **`(p'_i/p'_j)/(p_i/p_j)` inside `[(1−s)/(1+s), (1+s)/(1−s)]` for every PAIR — TRUE BY CONSTRUCTION because the renormalizer `Z` cancels out of a ratio of ratios, leaving exactly `f_i/f_j`.** ⛔ **NOT** `p'_i/p_i` inside `(1∓s)` — that quantity is `f_i/mean_f` and the claim is FALSE (R16); it is DELETED, not narrowed | `stableSampleByWeight` takes ONE draw regardless of weights (MEASURED: `let roll = rng.random() * total;` then a walk); the draw's VALUE is unchanged, only the selected index moves. ⚠ The argmax fallback (`rng` absent) is order-preserving under renormalization, so the no-rng path stays canonical |
| **the keyed race** `w * hash01(key)` | `demographicsResponses.selectResponse` · `sovereigntyMarketStage` · the `hash01` consumers | multiplicand on `entry.weight` — **the ONE idiom where a raw weight multiplicand is exactly proportional**, because the race is already `w · u` with `u` per-candidate | the **WIN-SHARE ODDS RATIO** between any two candidates over the hash population, same bound. ⚠ Stated as odds, not as a share ratio, for the same reason as row 1: only the odds form is normalization-free | the hash key is PER-CANDIDATE and independent of the weight, so the winner can change while the stream cannot. ⚠ CONSERVATIVE REINFORCEMENT applies where `w` is itself `clamp01`-saturated (§3b) |

| **guard chains** | `razingGate` · `ransomChoices` × 2 · `deliberationRead` | ⛔ NOWHERE — STAY-DETERMINISTIC (J-HB-6) | — | n/a |

⛔ **THE EXPONENT PROHIBITION IS NOW ENFORCEABLE, WHERE BEFORE IT WAS ONLY STATED.** The
prohibition below says a habit factor is never an exponent. The compile's own idiom
table then placed the factor on the pre-softmax score, **which at a softmax IS
exponentiation** (§3b's worked case). The application law is what makes the prohibition
true in substance rather than in wording, and the walker that enforces it is a source
scan asserting **no habit symbol appears upstream of a `softmaxWeights(` or `logit(`
call in any joined module** — a positional scan, because the prohibition is about
POSITION, and a scan for `Math.pow` alone would have missed every instance of the
defect it exists to catch.

⛔ **THE EXPONENT PROHIBITION, quoted from the site that ruled it.**
`demographicsResponses` carries a standing in-source determinism ruling: the race is
`w * u`, **never** `u ** (1/w)`, because `Math.pow` is implementation-approximated. **A
habit factor is a MULTIPLICAND ON A WEIGHT OR A SCORE. It is never an exponent, never
an argument to `Math.pow`, and never applied to a probability by exponentiation.** A
wave that finds itself wanting an exponent has found a site that does not fit and owes
a STOP.

### ⭐⭐ THE NAMED-DOMAIN TOTALITY CHECKLIST (refinement 17 — its own section, because
### the owner named these BY NAME and a row inside another table is a row that gets lost)

The directive's cohesion ruling names seven domains and demands that each be
dispositioned **BY NAME**, with the explicit instruction that a cost-argmax fork gets an
explicit **CONVERT / STAY / DEFER** call rather than being silently excluded, and that
**any named domain absent from the census gets its own measured row**. Every row below
was RE-MEASURED at the amendment; two of the compile's seven rows were wrong and are
struck at source (R11 and the trade split). ⭐⭐ **THE COUNT IS FOURTEEN AND THE WALKER
ASSERTION HAS EXACTLY ONE SPELLING (the P7 closure, CORRECTED UNDER J-HB-23).** The volume
previously stated the assertion three incompatible ways — *"all seven domains are
present"* here, *"the TWELVE named-domain rows of §3c asserted present"* in HB-1's
charter, and *"TWELVE named-domain rows"* in the footer — and then the polish's own

EXECUTED SELF-ASSERTION rather than a read-off.** Parsed from the table below at this

DYNAMICS 1 · GOALS 2 · GOAL ORIENTATION 1 = **14**. ⛔ **A shrink-only registry walker

AND ITS COUNT ARE ONE EDIT.** A row added without re-running the parse is how a

**THE ONE SPELLING, used verbatim everywhere it appears:**

> **`habitDomainChecklist.walker` ASSERTS THREE THINGS (J-HB-23).** They are three
> assertions rather than one because a dropped ROW, a dropped LABEL and a dropped
> OWNER-DOMAIN are three different failures and each must red its own line.
>

> COUNT.** The set of domain labels across those rows equals exactly, and only,
> **{ TRADE, FAITH, STRATEGY, ROUTE CREATION, ROUTE CHOICE, INTER-SETTLEMENT DYNAMICS,
> GOALS, GOAL ORIENTATION }** — eight labels, every one named in full here, asserted as a

> SEVEN spoken domains maps onto at least one label of (b), by this recorded mapping:
> *"trade"* → TRADE · *"faith"* → FAITH · *"strategy"* → STRATEGY · *"route creation"* →
> ROUTE CREATION · *"route choice"* → ROUTE CHOICE · *"dynamics between settlements"* →
> INTER-SETTLEMENT DYNAMICS · *"goals, and goal orientation"* → **BOTH `GOALS` AND `GOAL

⚠ The row count, the label set and the owner-domain mapping are THREE DIFFERENT
ASSERTIONS and all three are made — the row count catches a dropped row, the label set
catches a dropped or misspelled LABEL, the mapping catches a dropped OWNER-NAMED DOMAIN,
and no one of them can do another's job. Each row joins `habitForkRegistry.js`.

**THE DECOMPOSITION, AND IT SUMS.** FOURTEEN rows across EIGHT labels across the owner's
SEVEN named domains: TRADE splits THREE ways (partnerships, embargoes, caravan routing);
**FAITH splits THREE (the observance, the IMPOSITION, patrons + clergy — row 4b is the
third, it was minted at the polish, and it is precisely the row the old count missed)**;
ROUTE CREATION splits TWO (charter vs genesis); GOALS splits TWO (selection,
persistence-vs-abandonment); and STRATEGY, ROUTE CHOICE, INTER-SETTLEMENT DYNAMICS and

belief-routed, structurally, by the gate ladder (§2.2); the HABIT term is always
OWN-state and is therefore neither belief nor foreign truth.**

### 3d THE ANTICIPATION TERM — belief-routed, LEVEL ONE, one typed factor

**The believed-doctrine axis is a FOURTH SP-B SUBJECT FAMILY, and the recipe is
executed and quotable.** SP-B's extension recipe is five joins, zero new decay law,
zero new writer:

1. **A PURE, FLAGLESS FAMILY LEAF.** `beliefAxisSubjects.js` gains the doctrine
   derivation. Its header states the law verbatim: *"THIS LEAF IS PURE AND FLAGLESS. It
   reads no rule key; `beliefAxes.js` owns the one gate door and hands the resolved
   family gates down as data."*
2. **ONE MORE BY-NAME LINE IN `subjectAxesActive`** —
   `const doctrine = rules.believedDoctrineEnabled === true;` — beside the existing
   three. The frozen-list `.every()` hole is why the read is by name.
3. **ONE MORE DERIVATION IN `subjectGroundTruth`**, reached through `axisGroundTruth`,
   THE ONE TRUTH DOOR. ⚠ The habit ledger arrives as an OPTS MEMBER (`{ habitLedger }`),
   exactly as `religionState` does today — **the options object is built ONLY when the
   family is lit**, so a dark family does not so much as look at the truth container.
4. **ONE MORE ARM IN `foldSubjectAxes`'s droppable arms table**:
   `[gates.doctrine === true, 'doctrineBands', T.DOCTRINE_ADOPT]`.
5. **ONE `@property` ON `BeliefRecord`**, one member in `SUBJECT_AXIS_FIELDS`, one
   manifest key, one cert row.

**IT RIDES `BELIEF_TUNING`'s decay, its forgetting, and the `infoMode` gate FOR FREE.**
J-SP-1 is quoted and binding: *"There is NO believed-world module and NO second decay
law… A parallel believed-scarcity ledger, a second fold, or a second decay law anywhere
would be a design defect."* ⭐ **AND THE POLISH MAKES THAT SENTENCE TRUE RATHER THAN
ASPIRATIONAL (P4):** the shelf-life modulation composes into the EXISTING per-pair
`decayKeep01` argument that `adjustedDecayBase` already takes and already clamps to
`[-1, 1]` — a second signed contribution to a modifier that exists, not a parameter on a
constant that has none. **`BELIEF_TUNING.SILENCE_DECAY` is not edited, the three existing
SP-B families are untouched, and a contribution of 0 is the byte-identity anchor the
function already documents.**

**THE SHEET SHAPE — small, banded, stealable.** ⭐ **THE `law` MEMBER IS ADDED AT THE
POLISH (P4) and is one more key inside the family HB-6 already mints — never a fifth
subject family:**

```
doctrineBands: {
  law:   <a LAW_WORDS member>,      // the OBSERVER'S BELIEVED law band of the subject.
                                    // Truth side: lawWordFor(settlementAlignment(item,
                                    // worldState).lawfulness01) — MEASURED at HEAD, one
                                    // existing call, through THE ONE TRUTH DOOR.
                                    // Drop-when-absent like everything else: a court that
                                    // has never had a clear-eyed look carries no law
                                    // reading and contributes exactly 0 to decayKeep01.
  sheet: { <circumstanceClass>: { move: <actionToken>, hold: <holdRung> } }
}
```

The sheet holds at most TWELVE entries, each two words. Classes where the subject has no live stock are
ABSENT — **absence is a result** (the `outboundImpression` law), and it renders as "this
court has not yet shown its hand", never as a sheet of neutrals. The truth-side
derivation `doctrineSheetOf(ledger, actorId)` takes, per class with a live stock above
`SHEET_FLOOR`, the ARGMAX action with a codepoint tiebreak.

⛔ **THE SHEET IS MODAL, NOT DISTRIBUTIONAL, AND THAT IS A DESIGN DECISION.** A
distribution would be a float surface — unprintable under L5, unpinnable under the
no-decimal runtime rule, and unstealable in any narrative sense (a spy brings home a
sheet, not a histogram). The cost is real and recorded: two actions at 0.55 and 0.54
present as a confident single answer. The `hold` rung is what carries that honesty —
a near-tie reads `restrained`, and a `restrained` sheet line is explicitly weak
evidence.

**`HABIT_HOLD_BANDS` IS A BORROW, NOT A MINT (J-WR-10-B) — ⚠ AND THE BORROW IS
ONE-DIRECTIONAL, NOT A TWO-WAY EQUALITY (the M2 closure, R9).** The rungs are
`dispositionLedger`'s `CHANNEL_BANDS` — `restrained · measured · settled · marked ·
dominant` — because that ladder is ALREADY the banded prose projection of a learned
per-actor stock, which is exactly what this is. **The compile's recipe was
unimplementable as written**, three ways: MEASURED, `CHANNEL_BANDS` is declared `const`
(not exported), is an array of OBJECTS `{maxExclusive, name}` rather than strings, and
is ordered ASCENDING BY THRESHOLD — consumed as `CHANNEL_BANDS.find((band) => stock <
band.maxExclusive)?.name`, which works **only because the index IS the rank**, the exact
opposite of the compile's warning. The words are not codepoint-sorted either (d < m < m
< r < s). So:

```js
// habitVocabulary.js — the ONE-DIRECTIONAL borrow
import { DISPOSITION_CHANNEL_TUNING } from '../dispositionLedger.js';
export const HABIT_HOLD_BANDS = Object.freeze(
  DISPOSITION_CHANNEL_TUNING.BANDS.map((b) => String(b.name)),
);
export const HOLD_RANK = Object.freeze(
  Object.fromEntries(HABIT_HOLD_BANDS.map((name, i) => [name, i])),
);
```

**HB DERIVES; IT DOES NOT COPY.** There is no second array to keep equal to a first, so
there is nothing for a two-directional walker to compare and no way for the two to
drift. `DISPOSITION_CHANNEL_TUNING` is MEASURED as the exported surface
(`export const DISPOSITION_CHANNEL_TUNING = Object.freeze({ … BANDS: CHANNEL_BANDS })`),
so the import is legal today with no edit to `dispositionLedger`. **THE WALKER'S JOB
CHANGES ACCORDINGLY** and is now three assertions that are actually checkable: (1) the
projected array equals the literal five rungs, so a rename upstream reds HERE rather
than silently re-spelling a doctrine sheet; (2) `DISPOSITION_CHANNEL_TUNING.BANDS` is
**threshold-ASCENDING** (`b[i].maxExclusive < b[i+1].maxExclusive`), because the derived
rank is only meaningful while that holds — a reordering upstream would invert every
doctrine sheet in the world silently, and this is the assertion that catches it; (3) no
HB module spells a hold rung outside the vocabulary leaf. ⚠ The compile's warning is
INVERTED and the corrected form is worth keeping in the header: **here the index IS the
rank, and it is derived FROM the threshold order — never from a sort.**

**THE ANTICIPATION FACTOR — one term, level one, no regress.**

```
anticipationFactorFor(worldState, observerId, subjectId, cls, ownAction)
  → 1 unless habitAnticipationActive
  → 1 unless the observer's belief record for the subject carries doctrineBands[cls]
  → 1 + ANTICIPATION_SPAN * counterWeight(ownAction, believedMove) * holdRank01
```

`counterWeight` is a frozen per-vocabulary table mapping (their believed move, my
candidate) to `+1` (this counters what they will do), `-1` (this walks into it), or `0`
(orthogonal) — **authored per action vocabulary, never derived**, because "what counters
what" is design knowledge and not arithmetic. `holdRank01` scales by how firmly the
belief is held, so a `restrained` reading barely moves anything.

⭐⭐ **THE TABLE'S SHAPE IS BOUNDED BY THE PER-ACTION ARITY, AND THE SAVING IS LARGE
(P2/P9).** The candidate axis is not the vocabulary — **it is the vocabulary's DYADIC
subset**, because a monadic candidate has no counterpart to bind and never reaches
`anticipationFactorFor` at all. At site 7 that is one move:

```
counterWeight(STRATEGY_MOVES)  :  believedMove ∈ 11   ×   ownAction ∈ {deploy}   =  11 cells
                                  ⛔ NOT 11 × 11 = 121
counterWeight(pact answers)    :  believedAnswer ∈ 2  ×  ownAction ∈ {accept, refuse}  =  4 cells
```

⚠ **AND THE TOTALITY ASSERTION IS SCOPED TO EXACTLY THOSE CELLS.** An authored-cell
census over the full cross-product would be a fake obligation nobody could discharge —
110 of the 121 cells at site 7 are unreachable by construction, and a walker demanding
them would be demanding authored design knowledge about decisions the engine cannot take.
**The census denominator is `dyadicActions × believedVocabulary`, derived from the
registry row, never a hand-written grid.** ⛔ A missing cell inside that denominator is a
RED, never a zero default.

**⭐ THE LEVEL-ONE BOUND IS RULED AND ALREADY BUILT — copy it, do not re-argue it.**
`src/domain/worldPulse/outboundImpression.js` is the recursion law made code. Its own
words: *"Reading the counterpart's own belief record to answer 'what do they believe of
us' is not a model of a mind, it is omniscience with an extra step — and it would make
every court a perfect mind-reader of every other court, which is the exact failure the
belief partition exists to prevent. So this module holds a ZERO-IMPORT contract and
never receives a belief record at all."*

**THE HABIT APPLICATION IS EXACT.** A court's answer to *"what do they believe MY habits
are"* — the does-B-know-A-knows layer the owner named — comes from **the court's OWN
record of what it DID where that observer could see**, never by reading the observer's
belief row. Same zero-import contract, same NULL-on-no-evidence rule, same *"It MINTS NO
VOCABULARY, DELIBERATELY"*. **AND THE REGRESS STOPS THERE:** level two ("what do they
believe I believe about their habits") is NOT built, NOT approximated, and NOT reserved.
⚠ MEASURED and stated so no wave assumes otherwise: `secondOrderBeliefEnabled` returns
ZERO hits in `src` — IN-1's mirror has not landed. HB does not depend on it and does not
anticipate it.

⭐⭐ **WHY THE WAR CHOOSER IS NOW AVAILABLE, AND WHY IT IS THE RIGHT REPLACEMENT (A2).**
The compile treated the war chooser as reachable only by HB-5's habit read, and routed
anticipation elsewhere. **The owner's blanket queue sign-off RELEASED the war-chooser
spy-before-decision wiring by name (R1), so a belief-routed read at that block is
authorized ground, not a parked one** — and anticipation is precisely a
spy-before-decision read: it asks what this court believes the counterpart will do.
Landing it at the same block HB-5 already opens means the estate's most contended
surface is visited ONCE for three reads (habit, the signed spy read, and later the
anticipation multiplicand into an already-built product) rather than three times. ⚠ The
size discipline is unchanged and binding: HB-7's delta at that file is DECLARED against
HB-1's ratchet-down, and a wave that needs more STOPS.

### 3e THE DOCTRINE SHEET — dossier, tap product, planted doctrine

Three surfaces in build order. **Nothing about (1) and (2) needs (3)**, which matters
because (3) is blocked on SP-D (§2.2).

**(1) THE DOSSIER PROJECTION — reachable today.** The pattern is landed and complete:
`src/domain/display/settlementBeliefs.js` (the read model, fail-closed:
`if (!includeGroundTruth) return []; // player projection: beliefs are DM-only`) +
`src/components/map/BeliefDivergenceBand.jsx` (the UI, `const includeGroundTruth = tier
=== 'premium' || elevated;`, rendered ONLY inside the DM's Realm Inspector, never a
shared/public dossier or a free/anon PDF export, lazy inside an already-lazy chunk).

The doctrine sheet is **ONE MORE ROW FAMILY INSIDE THAT EXISTING BAND** — pills in the
same grammar, divergence prose in the same `<ul>`, the same premium/elevated gate, the
same self-gate on field presence, the same codepoint sort. **NO new panel, NO new tier
gate, NO new sort.** The divergence line writes itself and is the payoff sentence of the
whole program: *"believes they always march first; they have twice waited."*

⚠ **REFINEMENT 14 — OWN-SHEET TRUTH — lands HERE and nowhere else.** An actor's own
doctrine sheet IS its own truth (the settlement knows itself), so the DM's view of a
settlement's OWN sheet reads the ledger directly, while its view of what OTHERS believe
reads `doctrineBands`. Two rows, one panel, and the difference between them is the whole
drama. ⚠ **The DM-only fail-closed default is load-bearing and inherited, not
re-litigated:** a player who could read doctrine sheets would have perfect
counter-intelligence for free.

**(2) THE ES TAP PRODUCT — by ACCESS DEPTH (owner addition H), blocked on SP-D.**
`TAP_LEVELS = ['beliefs','delta','performance']` with
`TAP_DEPTH = { performance: 0, beliefs: 1, delta: 2 }` — ⚠ the array is CODEPOINT-sorted
and the SEMANTIC depth is `performance < beliefs < delta`; a consumer using the array
index would rank an open visitor's hearsay above an embedded agent's read. The doctrine
mapping falls out of own-sheet truth exactly:

| Tap | Access | What the doctrine product returns |
|---|---|---|
| `performance` | a `face:'declared'` stop, and EVERY standoff read | **THE PERFORMED SHEET** — what the court is willing to be SEEN doing, poisoned by `perfPoison01` and **REPLACED, line by line, by any live planted doctrine** the host is asserting |
| `beliefs` | a `face:'covert'` stop the agent has ENTERED | **THE REAL SHEET** — own-sheet truth, so the host's true doctrine IS its self-belief. Cap 1.0 only at the target stop |
| `delta` | embedded WITH a live inside asset | **THE DISINFORMATION DETECTOR** — both layers plus the plant exposed: *"they say they always march; they have twice waited."* The ONLY tap that distinguishes a planted sheet from a real one |

**`doctrine` joins the ES `legRefs` closed set as a SEVENTH member.** ⚠ ES §1 enumerates
SIX EXACTLY and the mint REFUSES anything outside them, so this is an ES-SIDE vocabulary
change and a seam obligation on that volume (§8 row 3), landed in the SAME commit as
HB-8's tap arm — never smuggled. Products write through `reconcileBelief` as synthetic
BeliefReports (J-ES-5), never through `applyBeliefOverrides`.

**(3) PLANTED DOCTRINE — the road is RULED and the wrong road is a SILENT DROP.**

⛔ **THE PLANT OVERRIDE ROAD CANNOT CARRY DOCTRINE.**
`exactKeys(override, PAID_PLANT_OVERRIDE_KEYS)` is an **exact-six-key equality**; an
envelope carrying a seventh field makes `commissionedPlantAt` return `null` and **the
entire commission is dropped with no error.** That is the ghost-write class, and it is
the sharpest single hazard in this program's integration surface. HB-8 pins the
NEGATIVE: an override carrying a doctrine field must be asserted to return null.

✅ **PLANTED DOCTRINE RIDES THE REPORT ROAD** — a synthetic BeliefReport asserting a
`doctrineBands` value the liar wants believed, with the disinfo LEDGER row
(`spatialLedgers.disinfo`, `plant:*` namespace) carrying the lineage for exposure
accounting. It inherits, for free: one writer (the information-statecraft family head),
seed/propagate/corroborate/contradict/expose, the credibility closure (a discredited
liar's plant is worth less), and the exposure arm's `EXPOSE_GRIEVANCE_W` resentment plus
its credibility charge — **the cost of being caught forging your own reputation.**

⛔ The ephemeral in-errand PICTURE channel (`ENVOY_PICTURE_PLANT_FIELDS`) is the WAR
lane's and is NOT touched by an information-side product — J-INA-2's two-channel ruling,
extended by ES §3.6.

⚠ **THE TRANSPORT PROBLEM IS INHERITED, NOT NEW.** MEASURED: `pulseKernel` calls
`advanceInformationStatecraft` WITHOUT `commissionedPlants`, and NOTHING in `src` reads
`metadata.plant` (executed grep: zero). Both consumers are fed only by tests. The ruled
road is CR-FP-4: each consumer reads the PRIOR tick's applied `brokerage_plant` events at
its own head (zero new keys, zero kernel edits, law-M one-week lag), with the
`pendingPlants` conditional deposit as fallback and a chair-signed kernel seam as the
STOP. **A planted DOCTRINE sheet inherits the SAME problem and the SAME ruled road** and
mints no third answer.

⭐⭐ **REFINEMENT 16'S SECOND HALF LANDS IN THE ES SEAM, NOT ONLY IN THE CURVE — THE
INTELLIGENCE SHELF LIFE, stated where a spy product is priced. ⚠⚠ REBUILT ON THE
BELIEVED CLOCK AT THE POLISH (P4); the truth-clock version is STRUCK at §3b and the
table below is re-grounded on it.** §3b establishes that the target's OWN learning rate
and half-life are modulated by its TRUE law word, and that **an observer's believed
doctrine loses CONFIDENCE at a rate modulated by the observer's BELIEVED law band of that
target** — folded into `beliefMap`'s existing per-PAIR `decayKeep01` modifier, which is
why it costs no machinery. **THE CURRENCY IS CONFIDENCE, NOT VALUE:** a stolen sheet does
not turn into a different sheet, it stays exactly what it said and stops being worth
acting on.

| The observer BELIEVES the target is | The target's own memory (TRUE law word) | The stolen sheet's confidence | What it is worth |
|---|---|---|---|
| `lawful` | if truly lawful: slow accretion, long retention | decays SLOWLY (`decayKeep01` positive) | ⭐ **DURABLE TREASURE** — worth a deep tap and worth paying for. ⚠ **AND WORTH FORGING:** see the deception row |
| `balanced` | the base curve | the base curve (`decayKeep01` contribution 0 — the byte-identity anchor) | ordinary intelligence with an ordinary shelf life |
| `lawless` | if truly lawless: hot learning, fast forgetting | decays FAST (`decayKeep01` negative) | ⚠ **FRESH FISH** — a tap on a court you believe chaotic is a PERISHABLE purchase, and buying one early is buying a sheet you will stop trusting before you use it |
| ⭐ **anything, PLANTED** | whatever it actually is | decays at the rate of the PLANTED image | ⭐⭐ **THE DECEPTION ROW — the thing the truth-clock version could not have.** A court that cultivates a chaotic image makes everyone's intelligence about it rot in their hands, **including intelligence that is perfectly accurate.** A court that plants a LAWFUL image makes STALE intelligence feel durable — the sharper attack, because the mark keeps acting on a sheet it has stopped checking. It rides the plant road HB-8 already builds; no fourth road, no new commission shape |

**PREDICTABILITY BECOMES A CULTURAL TRAIT, AND BOTH LEGIBILITY AND ILLEGIBILITY BECOME
WEAPONS — AND SO DOES THE APPEARANCE OF EITHER.** The lawful court is reliable and
readable, and therefore anticipatable, which is §0.4's loop biting exactly where the
directive says it should. The chaotic court is erratic and unreadable and pays for it in
its own worse memory. ⭐ **The court that is neither but is BELIEVED to be one of them
pays nothing and collects the whole discount** — which is the first place in this estate
where information ABOUT information is itself a weapon, and it is a designed consequence
of the chair's P4 ruling rather than a side effect of it. ⛔ **THE PRICING RULE THIS PUTS
ON HB-8, now correctly keyed:** the tap product's value statement in the dossier reads
**the buyer's BELIEVED law band of the subject** — because that is what the buyer's own
sheet will decay on — and the divergence line is where the truth eventually contradicts
it. A tap product that presents a believed-lawless court's sheet with the same confidence
as a believed-lawful one's is lying by omission. **Composes with refinement 8's seat shock
with no interaction term** — a new seat on a truly lawless throne forgets twice, which is
correct and needs no arithmetic to say so.

**REFINEMENT 13 — OBSERVED-DOCTRINE ACCRUAL — COSTS NOTHING, and that is the finding.**
The requirement is that believed doctrine is never free: it accrues slowly from PUBLICLY
OBSERVED actions, hop-worn, and the ES tap JUMPS it toward truth. **SP-B's fold already
does exactly this.** `foldSubjectAxes` adopts truth only when `bestFidelity(reports) >=
DOCTRINE_ADOPT`, where `bestFidelity` takes the MAXIMUM `accuracy01` across reports —
*"one clear-eyed witness is what changes a court's mind"*. Set `DOCTRINE_ADOPT` HIGH and:
ordinary news-road reports (hop-worn, low accuracy) rarely clear it, so doctrine
converges SLOWLY from public actions; a tap product (accuracy near 1.0 at a `beliefs` or
`delta` tap) clears it immediately, so espionage JUMPS it. **The counterforce is emergent
before espionage lights, and spying stays the sharp edge — with ZERO new machinery, one
tuning constant, and no second accrual road.**

### 3f THE DEVIATION DAMPER — the self-balancing loop, keyed on awareness

The owner's counterforce: *"a watched actor's deviate-from-pattern candidate gains weight
with counterintelligence awareness."*

**THE IMPLEMENTATION MINTS NO CANDIDATE.** Adding **one additional** "do something
unexpected" move to every action vocabulary would be a new option at every site, a new
prose obligation, a new close, and a new dead-band risk. Instead the damper narrows the
actor's own habit span:

```
effectiveSpan = HABIT_SPAN * (1 - DEVIATE_W * awareness01(worldState, actorId))
```

A watched actor follows its habit LESS, so every non-modal candidate gains RELATIVE
weight — which is the owner's sentence, expressed as relative weight. No new option, no
new draw, no new vocabulary. Dark or unaware, `awareness01` is 0 and the span is
untouched.

**`awareness01` READS ONLY LIVE SIGNALS, AND THE ONES IT NEEDS ARE MEASURED PRESENT:**

```
awareness01 = clamp01( sightPressure01(worldState, actorId)      // who is watching me
                     * ownSecrecyLevel01(worldState, actorId) )  // do I have eyes to notice
```

Both come from the ONE live watched-ness substrate:
`spatialLedgers.sightPostures[watcher][target].fidelity01` and
`spatialLedgers.secrecyPostures[id].level01`, written by `informationStatecraft.js` and
read today through `makeSightFn` / `sightFidelityOf`, all under `infoStatecraftEnabled`.

⚠⚠ **THE OMNISCIENCE HOLE, NAMED AND CLOSED.** "Who is watching me" is a TRUTH read over
other actors' postures. Taken bare, it would hand every court perfect counterintelligence
for free — the exact failure the belief partition exists to prevent, and it would make
the damper a reward for being spied on rather than a reward for VIGILANCE. **THE CURE IS
THE PRODUCT FORM:** the truth of being watched is multiplied by the actor's OWN secrecy
investment. A court that has bought no counterintelligence has `awareness01 = 0` and
learns nothing about its watchers no matter how many there are. **Awareness is a
PURCHASED CAPABILITY**, and the damper is a return on that purchase.

⚠⚠ **THREE PREMISES THE ES PROGRAM DESIGNED FOR THIS ARE REFUTED AT HEAD. A wave that
assumes them ships a dead arm.**
- `suspicionOf` — IN-3's court-level counterintel read: **ZERO hits in `src` and
  `tests`.** Docs-only.
- `counterIntelEnabled` — **ZERO hits in `src`**; absent from
  `DEFAULT_SIMULATION_RULES` and from `ENGINE_GATED_VIRTUAL_RULE_KEYS`. IN-3 has not
  landed.
- `overdueForeignNotables` / `recentCovertHolds` — the world-side feeds for
  `wariness01Core` exist only as JSDoc parameter names inside `espionageMath.js`.

**CONSEQUENCE: the loop closes TODAY on the sight/secrecy posture pair alone, and it
CANNOT close on `suspicionOf`.** When IN-3 eventually lands, `awareness01` composes it as
a THIRD term through the one derivation — a widening, never a second spelling. The seam
is pre-pinned from both sides (§8 row 6).

⚠ `infoStatecraftEnabled` dark ⇒ no posture ledgers ⇒ `awareness01` is 0 ⇒ the damper is
inert and DECLARED-DEGRADED, never silently absent. The receipt names the missing family
— the SP-C absent-not-zero idiom.

### 3g RECEIPTS — habit as a named reason family in the finite semantics

**THE RECEIPT VOCABULARY** — one frozen closed set, one shape:

```
{ kind: 'habit',
  circumstanceClass: <one of 12>,
  action:            <the site's own action token>,
  hold:              <one of HABIT_HOLD_BANDS>,
  source:            'own' | 'anticipated' }
```

**FIVE LAWS, each with its enforcement:**

**1. THE SHEET SPEAKS RUNGS, NEVER COUNTS.** ⚠ The directive's illustrative sentence —
*"as it has thrice before when granaries ran thin"* — is REALIZED as a RUNG phrase, not
a tally: *"as this court reliably does when its granaries run thin."* The reason is
recorded rather than assumed: a tally is a number in prose, the estate's
`proseNumerics` detector is a live scanner, and the stock is a decayed continuous value
whose "count" is not an integer anyone could honestly print. **A habit has a FIRMNESS,
not a history of tallies.** This is a deliberate, vetoable narrowing of the directive's
phrasing and is recorded as J-HB-8.
⚠ **CORRECTED AT THE AMENDMENT (R8): `proseNumerics` IS NOT RED AT BASE.** The compile's
note ("a chair STOP still standing, 401 baseline vs 410 live") is DISCHARGED. MEASURED
at HEAD: `tests/lint/proseNumerics.test.js` carries `// CR-FP-2 — THE ONE RULED
RE-RECORD (FP cycle 1 close). 404 -> 413.` and `const REVIEWED_TOTAL_CEILING = 413;`,
and the baseline file holds exactly **413** rows. **THE LIVE DISCIPLINE — which is what
an HB wave actually meets — is stricter, not looser:** the scanner is an EXACT-EQUALITY
ceiling in BOTH directions with the in-file instruction "never raise a ceiling", so an
HB wave that adds prose **re-records its own delta against 413 with an authored
attribution naming the wave**. It inherits no red and it raises no ceiling. ⚠ An
implementer sent chasing a red that does not exist would attribute correct new prose to
a phantom and leave the real re-record unwritten — which is why this correction is made
at the source sentence rather than footnoted.

**2. NO FLOAT REACHES PROSE (L5).** `habitStrength` is a control scalar and never enters
a receipt, a news beat, or a dossier pill. The reader learns the court's character, not
its coefficients. The runtime no-decimal pin covers composed output, because source scans
cannot see composers.

**3. ONE BAND PER CLAUSE, COMPARISONS IN WORDS.** Banding is LOSSY: two stocks
straddling a rung edge share a rung, and printing both mints a visible contradiction.
The divergence line therefore says *"reads them as decisive; they have grown
restrained"* and never juxtaposes two same-rung readings as if they differed.

**4. THE NEWS ADDRESS LAW, discharged for the one new kind.** HB mints exactly ONE
Herald kind: **`doctrine_shifted`** — an actor's own doctrine crossing a band in a
class. It is minted through `bandCrossingReceipt`, **THE ONE CROSSING-RECEIPT GRAMMAR**
(`bandedStock.js`), which is FAIL-CLOSED by construction: every field is validated,
tokens must match `/^[a-z][a-z_]*$/`, the tick must be a non-negative INTEGER, and a
non-crossing (`from === to`) THROWS — *"emitting one for a non-event is how a feed
becomes wallpaper."* **L5 is therefore STRUCTURAL here: a float cannot reach prose
through this grammar because a float cannot enter it.**
⚠ THE TOKEN-SHAPE TRAP: that regex rejects Title-Case (`PROSPERITY_LADDER`) and hyphens
(`CAUSE_CLASS_IDS`). **Every habit vocabulary token is `lower_snake`** — all twelve
classes above already are, and any borrowed action token that is not cannot enter the
receipt grammar and must be mapped, never re-spelled.

The kind lands the **FIVE JOINS** in its mint commit (L6): the annex-verbatim pool row ·
the registry row with `requiredSlots` + a slotless fallback · `WHAT_PHRASES` ·
`EXACT_SECTION`/`sectionAuthority` (the knowledge desk) · the full address chain (id +
settlementIds + typed action + recorded reasons + audience). Missing identities return
null, never throw. It gets its OWN walker file — never rows in a foreign walker.
⚠ **PAIR-ONLY ≠ SLOTLESS:** a slotless fallback is REQUIRED, and "empty interp returns
null" is a WRONG pin (11 of 20 TRADE pools have no slotless variant — the recorded trap).

**5. THE REASON CLAUSE IS ADDITIVE AT EVERY EXISTING RECEIPT.** At a joined chooser,
habit appends ONE reason row to the receipt the site already emits. It never replaces a
reason, never reorders the list, and — dark — appends nothing, so the receipt is
byte-identical. ⚠ Several sites SLICE their reasons (`compactOutcomeForHistory` keeps
`reasons` **SLICED TO 4**), so a habit reason can silently displace a causal one in the
pulse record. **The habit reason sorts LAST in the site's own ordering**, so it is the
first thing dropped by a slice rather than the thing that drops a cause — declared,
pinned, and the reason the ordering is not left to chance.

**THE DM-EDIT STORY (spine req 14), discharged for the family:** habit state is
ENGINE-ONLY and carries no DM verb in v1 — a DM who could hand-edit a doctrine sheet
would be authoring the past, which is the lived-history law's exact prohibition. The DM's
surface is the DOSSIER (read), the divergence band (read), and — through the ES program's
existing `DISPATCH_CONFIRMATION_MISSION` proposal verb — the ability to GO AND LOOK.
Recorded, with the rationale, in every wave's ledger row.

and J-HB-6 protects only the second; and the TRADE ROUTING close. ⭐⭐ **PLUS THE THREE
CLOSES THE ACTION-COVERAGE LAW PUT ON THE BOOKS AT THE POLISH (R18)** — the generosity
verdict close (site 2), the intervention engage/decline close (site 10) and the
sovereignty offer-posture close (site 12), each with its `closeOwed` sentence already
written at §3a's coverage table; converting them is what moves those three sites DEFER →
LEARN. ⚠ **AND THE NINE STRATEGY ACTIONS WITHOUT A CLOSE ARE HB-9's LARGEST HONEST GAP** —
`hold`'s ABSTENTION close in particular is a close-shape this estate has never minted, and
HB-9 either mints it or leaves the row on the books saying so. Convert what converts and
shrink the DEFER list; ⛔ **site 14 is STRUCK (R13) and its "wire if SP-D landed" clause is
DELETED** — `deliberationRead` is a guard chain, not a cadence fork, and no SP-D landing
changes that. The certification rows for all four
flags gain their dispositive literals; the HB ENVELOPES (habit-row density per actor,
class occupancy mix, hold-rung mix, doctrine-sheet coverage, plant share, lapse rate —
each INTERVAL_WEEKS-derived, each with an executed mutant). ⭐ **THE DEFER LIST THAT
SURVIVES THIS WAVE IS THE PROGRAM'S HONEST GAP** and it is published **in
`habitForkRegistry.js` itself, which is a repo file a reader can grep** — ⚠ **CORRECTED AT
THE POLISH (P8): NOT in "the volume's §7 rows", which are five chair questions and were
never a home for a deferral book.** The registry IS the publication; the volume points at
it. Req 13: declared-empty; req 14: engine-only.

**Wave count: 10.** Dependency spine: HB-0 (free) → HB-1 (free) → HB-2 (flag 1) → HB-3 →
HB-4 → HB-5 (⭐ JOINT with the signed war-chooser wiring) → HB-6 (flag 2; needs SP-B,
LANDED) → HB-7 (flag 3) → HB-8 (flag 4; tap arm needs SP-D) → HB-9. **No wave edits
`pulseKernel.js` or `applyWorldPulse.js`** (banked, zero headroom — L1); every mount is a
site's own stage or the lifecycle host.

## §5 QUEUE INSERTION — the amendment the chair lands in DESIGN_FP_ARCHITECTURE

**THE SEQUENCING RECOMMENDATION, with its rationale.** HB lands **after the ES spine, as
one family, JOINT with the signed war-chooser wiring, and BEFORE the
instrument-hardening wave and the soak.** Four reasons, in order of force:

1. **AFTER THE ES SPINE, because the tap machinery must exist before the doctrine sheet
   can be a spy product.** HB-8's tap arm is gated on `espionageActive`, which is gated on
   `errandSpineEnabled` (SP-D), which has not landed. Landing HB earlier does not break
   anything — HB-8's tap arm simply ships dark-forever — but it would spend the wave's
   verification budget on an arm nobody can reach, and it would put the doctrine sheet's
   sharpest surface in a commit whose fences cannot see it.
2. **JOINT WITH THE WAR-CHOOSER WIRING, because they retrofit the same frozen surface.**
   The owner's blanket sign-off (2026-08-05) released CR-ES-4's deferred arm and queued it
   "after the ES spine as its own wave". HB-5 needs the same 812-line file, the same
   candidate-scoring block, the same size-ratchet negotiation, and the same
   CHECK-GIT-FIRST window. Two waves would pay twice and would leave the chooser in an
   intermediate state nobody designed.
3. **BEFORE THE INSTRUMENT-HARDENING WAVE**, because HB adds THREE new instrument
   families — the chooser-totality walker, the fork registry, and the **HB `Bands:`/§8.2
   reconciliation walker** (⚠ the LAST residual of the struck §7 spelling, corrected here;
   §7 is chair questions and is never a reconciliation target — J-HB-21) — and hardening
   them in the same pass as their siblings is one review rather than two.
4. **BEFORE THE SOAK, necessarily**, because habit's entire tuning family is owner-signed
   AT the soak redo and the soak is where its envelopes are first measured. A habit
   program landing after the soak would owe a second soak.

⚠ **THE ONE ORDERING RISK, stated:** HB-6 needs SP-B (LANDED, 2026-08-05) and HB-8's tap
needs SP-D (NOT landed). If SP-D slips past the soak, HB-8 ships with two of its four
arms permanently dark. That is acceptable and is DECLARED in the cert row; it is not a
reason to hold the family.

**The following text folds into `docs/DESIGN_FP_ARCHITECTURE.md`. Each clause names its
landing section. It is written to be landed VERBATIM.**

1. **§3 THE FLAG FAMILY — four new rows:**
   `| N+1 | habitConditioningEnabled | HB-2 | the habit ledger: per (actor ×
   circumstanceClass × action) learned stocks, the pledge book, credit at close, and the
   habit term at every joined chooser |`

   doctrineBands, an observer's believed doctrine sheet for a subject |`

   — and the existing-flags paragraph gains: "`believedDoctrineEnabled` requires
